This story assembles `load` and `fetch` and proves the seams between them and the dispatcher. Each failure writes nothing to standard output, writes exactly one diagnostic line to standard error, and leaves stored rates untouched. Any seam that breaks this is fixed here.\n\n## Behavior\n\n| Trigger | Exit | Stdout | Stderr | Database |\n|---------|------|--------|--------|----------|\n| `fxrates load <path>` where `<path>` does not exist or cannot be read | 1 | empty | one line | unchanged |\n| `fxrates fetch` where `ecb.download_history()` raises `OperationalError` | 1 | empty | one line | unchanged |\n\nTests replace `ecb.download_history` with a stub; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-OperationalFailures.md ===\n# FEATURE: Operational Failures\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Proves that an unreadable load file and a failed fetch download each exit 1 with one stderr line, empty stdout and an unchanged database. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-LoadAtomic.md, FEATURE-Fetch.md, FEATURE-CliFoundation.md |\n| Provides | fxrates exit code 1 diagnostics |\n| Consumes | fxrates load, fxrates fetch |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nOperational failures are exit code `1`: a `load` file that cannot be read, and a `fetch` download that fails. This story assembles `load` and `fetch` and proves the seams between them and the dispatcher. Each failure writes nothing to standard output, writes exactly one diagnostic line to standard error, and leaves stored rates untouched. Any seam that breaks this is fixed here.\n\n## Behavior\n\n| Trigger | Exit | Stdout | Stderr | Database |\n|---------|------|--------|--------|----------|\n| `fxrates load <path>` where `<path>` does not exist or cannot be read | 1 | empty | one line | unchanged |\n| `fxrates fetch` where `ecb.download_history()` raises `OperationalError` | 1 | empty | one line | unchanged |\n\nTests replace `ecb.download_history` with a stub; no test opens a network connection.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC failures-load-unreadable ===\nIntent: Loading a path that does not exist exits 1 with empty stdout and exactly one stderr line, and the stored rates are the same before and after.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    missing = os.path.join(tmp, \"no-such-file.csv\")\n    with db_session() as db:\n        before = [(r.date, r.currency, r.rate) for r in db.rates.list_all()]\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", missing)\n        assert code == 1\n        assert out == \"\"\n        assert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n        after = [(r.date, r.currency, r.rate) for r in db.rates.list_all()]\n        assert after == before\n=== END AC failures-load-unreadable ===\n\n=== AC failures-load-directory ===\nIntent: Loading a path that is a directory is also unreadable and exits 1 with empty stdout, leaving stored rates unchanged.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport tempfile\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    with db_session() as db:\n        before = [(r.date, r.currency, r.rate) for r in db.rates.list_all()]\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", tmp)\n        assert code == 1\n        assert out == \"\"\n        assert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n        after = [(r.date, r.currency, r.rate) for r in db.rates.list_all()]\n        assert after == before\n=== END AC failures-load-directory ===\n\n=== AC failures-fetch-download ===\nIntent: When the stubbed download raises OperationalError, fetch exits 1 with empty stdout and one stderr line, and stored rates are unchanged.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport ecb\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ntry:\n    from cli.errors import OperationalError\nexcept ImportError:\n    from cli import OperationalError\n\n\ndef failing_download():\n    raise OperationalError(\"download failed\")\n\n\noriginal = ecb.download_history\necb.download_history = failing_download\ntry:\n    with db_session() as db:\n        before = [(r.date, r.currency, r.rate) for r in db.rates.list_all()]\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"fetch\")\n        assert code == 1\n        assert out == \"\"\n        assert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n        after = [(r.date, r.currency, r.rate) for r in db.rates.list_all()]\n        assert after == before\nfinally:\n    ecb.download_history = original\n=== END AC failures-fetch-download ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output.\n- A failing command writes exactly one diagnostic line to standard error.\n- No test opens a network connection; the download is always stubbed.\n- A failure never deletes or alters stored rates.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Health.md ===\n# FEATURE: Health\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Serves GET /health returning status ok without touching the database. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-WebFoundation.md |\n| Provides | GET /health |\n| Consumes | app_server.main.create_app |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nA liveness endpoint. default `8000`. A non-integer port is rejected earlier by usage validation with exit 2. |\n| Foreground | The command runs in the foreground until stopped. |\n| SIGTERM | uvicorn shuts down gracefully and the process exits 0. |\n| Stdout | Nothing is required. |\n| Schema | Serving never initializes the schema. |\n\nThe handler is `cli/commands/serve.py` with `run(args) -> int`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC serve-health-reachable ===\nIntent: Starting fxrates serve on a free local port makes GET /health answer 200 with the status ok body at 127.0.0.1 within a deadline, and the child is always terminated and reaped.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport signal\nimport socket\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nimport time\nimport urllib.request\n\nsock = socket.socket()\nsock.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\nport = sock.getsockname()[1]\nsock.close()\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    env = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": os.path.join(tmp, \"serve.db\")}\n    log = open(os.path.join(tmp, \"server.log\"), \"w+\")\n    child = subprocess.Popen(\n        [\"./fxrates\", \"serve\", \"--port\", str(port)],\n        stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env,\n    )\n    try:\n        url = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health\"\n        deadline = time.time() + 30\n        status = None\n        body = None\n        while time.time() < deadline:\n            if child.poll() is not None:\n                break\n            try:\n                with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=2) as r:\n                    status, body = r.status, json.loads(r.read())\n                break\n            except Exception:\n                time.sleep(0.25)\n        log.seek(0)\n        print(log.read(), file=sys.stderr)\n        assert child.poll() is None, \"server exited before answering\"\n        assert status == 200\n        assert body == {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n    finally:\n        if child.poll() is None:\n            child.terminate()\n        try:\n            child.wait(timeout=15)\n        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n            child.kill()\n            child.wait()\n        log.close()\n=== END AC serve-health-reachable ===\n\n=== AC serve-sigterm-clean ===\nIntent: Sending SIGTERM to a running server stops it within a deadline with exit status 0.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport signal\nimport socket\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nimport time\nimport urllib.request\n\nsock = socket.socket()\nsock.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\nport = sock.getsockname()[1]\nsock.close()\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    env = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": os.path.join(tmp, \"serve.db\")}\n    child = subprocess.Popen(\n        [\"./fxrates\", \"serve\", \"--port\", str(port)],\n        stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=env,\n    )\n    try:\n        url = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health\"\n        deadline = time.time() + 30\n        ready = False\n        while time.time() < deadline and child.poll() is None:\n            try:\n                with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=2) as r:\n                    ready = r.status == 200\n                break\n            except Exception:\n                time.sleep(0.25)\n        assert ready, \"server never became ready\"\n        child.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)\n        code = child.wait(timeout=20)\n        err = child.stderr.read().decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\")\n        print(err, file=sys.stderr)\n        assert code == 0\n    finally:\n        if child.poll() is None:\n            child.kill()\n            child.wait()\n        child.stderr.close()\n=== END AC serve-sigterm-clean ===\n\n=== AC serve-bad-port-usage ===\nIntent: A non-integer --port is a usage error that exits 2 with empty stdout and never starts a server.\nRequires: executable=python3;
